2397: ## 109. Load test model
2398: 
2399: The concurrent-user validation includes at least.
2400: 
2401: ```text
2402: Scenario A - Start burst
2403: Scenario B - Steady autosave
2404: Scenario C - Navigation and read burst
2405: Scenario D - Submission burst
2406: Scenario E - Recovery burst
2407: Scenario F - Mixed realistic exam behavior
2408: ```
2409: 
2410: A generic concurrent HTTP-client test is insufficient.
2411: 
2412: ---
2413: 
2414: ## 110. Start burst test
2415: 
2416: Recommended test.
2417: 
2418: ```text
2419: 5,000 prepared StudentAttempts
2420: 5,000 authenticated users
2421: Start requests released within a narrow interval
2422: snapshot_status = READY
2423: No preparation activity during test
2424: ```
2425: 
2426: Measure p50, p95, p99, error rate, database CPU, database connections, lock waits, WAL rate, application CPU, GC, and request queue depth.
2427: 
2428: Acceptance gate.
2429: 
2430: ```text
2431: 5,000 concurrent users
2432: Applicable p95 under 100 ms
2433: Greater than 99% submissions
2434: Less than 1% exam failure
2435: ```
2436: 
2437: The exact load profile and hardware are retained with the test evidence.
2438: 
2439: ---
2440: 
2441: ## 111. Autosave burst test
2442: 
2443: Model 5,000 active attempts with each user saving according to effective autosave policy, randomized interaction timing, occasional duplicate retry, and occasional temporary network failure.
2444: 
2445: Measure response-save p95 and p99, database write throughput, row-lock contention, deadlocks, connection saturation, and error and retry amplification.
2446: 
2447: The test demonstrates that retries do not become a retry storm.
2448: 
2449: ---
2450: 
2451: ## 112. Submission burst test
2452: 
2453: Model 5,000 active attempts reaching terminal submission within a short interval with some requests duplicated, some delayed, and some sessions expired.
2454: 
2455: Verify one SUBMITTED state, no duplicate evaluation trigger, no lost response mutation beyond the accepted race policy, and no cross-user contamination.
2456: 
2457: ---
2458: 
2459: ## 113. Recovery burst test
2460: 
2461: Model 5,000 active students with simulated API restart or connection loss, all recovering within a narrow interval.
2462: 
2463: Verify same attempt, same snapshot, same answers, same timer and deadline, no new attempt, and no duplicate session explosion.
2464: 
2465: ---
2466: 
2467: ## 114. Failure injection
2468: 
2469: P5 tests failures such as PostgreSQL transient failure, Redis unavailable, one application instance restart, worker crash during PREPARING, worker crash after snapshot rows but before READY, network timeout during response save, duplicate submit, expired token during runtime, and stale tab.
2470: 
2471: Correctness survives these events without manual database edits.
2472: 
2473: ---
2474:

2476: 
2477: Critical case.
2478: 
2479: ```text
2480: Worker starts
2481: student_attempt = PREPARING

1999: ## 83. Runtime authorization matrix
2000: 
2001: | Action | Student | Staff | Reviewer | Admin |
2002: |---|---|---|---|---|
2003: | Start own scheduled attempt | Yes | According to permission | According to permission | According to permission |
2004: | Read own attempt | Yes | According to permission | According to permission | According to permission |
2005: | Save own response | Yes | According to permission | According to permission | According to permission |
2006: | Submit own attempt | Yes | According to permission | According to permission | According to permission |
2007: | Read another student's attempt | No | Permission required | Permission required | Permission required |
2008: | Modify published snapshot | No | No | No | No |
2009: | Trigger preparation | No | Permission required | Permission required | Permission required |
2010: | Force operational recovery | No | Permission required | Permission required | Permission required |
2011: 
2012: The exact permission codes are derived from the Identity permission catalogue and are not invented in Delivery.
2013: 
2014: ---
2015: 
2016: ## 84. Database query rules
2017: 
2018: P5 repositories filter by attempt ID, filter by authenticated user, use indexes, use projections where practical, avoid N+1 question loading, avoid loading the entire QuestionBank, and avoid cross-module entity graphs.
2019: 
2020: For question delivery, prefer an explicit query plan that joins the snapshot rows and only the authorized QuestionVersion fields.
2021: 
2022: Do not expose JPA entities directly from controllers.

21: /**
22:  * Reusable base persistence support for the platform's standard entity fields
23:  * ({@code specs/09-P0-Foundation-V1.5.md §22}, {@code specs/04-Database-Schema-V1.5.md §6–§7}).
24:  *
25:  * <p>This is a {@link MappedSuperclass}: it contributes fields only to entities that choose to
26:  * extend it. It never forces audit columns into tables that are not specified to use them.
27:  *
28:  * <p>Physical constraints (NOT NULL, defaults) remain owned by Flyway migrations. The database
29:  * is the final integrity boundary.
30:  *
31:  * <p>Timestamps are stored as {@link Instant} (UTC) and mapped to {@code TIMESTAMPTZ}.
32:  */
33: @MappedSuperclass
34: @EntityListeners(AuditingEntityListener.class)
35: public abstract class BaseEntity {
36: 
37:     @Id
38:     @GeneratedValue(strategy = GenerationType.UUID)
39:     @Column(name = "id", nullable = false, updatable = false)
40:     private UUID id;
41: 
42:     @Enumerated(EnumType.STRING)
43:     @Column(name = "status", nullable = false)
44:     private Status status = Status.ACTIVE;
45: 
46:     @CreatedBy
47:     @Column(name = "created_by", updatable = false)
48:     private UUID createdBy;
49: 
50:     @CreatedDate
51:     @Column(name = "created_on", updatable = false)
52:     private Instant createdOn;
53: 
54:     @LastModifiedBy
55:     @Column(name = "updated_by")
56:     private UUID updatedBy;
57: 
58:     @LastModifiedDate
59:     @Column(name = "updated_on")
60:     private Instant updatedOn;
61: 
62:     public UUID getId() {
63:         return id;
64:     }
65: 
66:     public void setId(UUID id) {
67:         this.id = id;
68:     }
69: 
70:     public Status getStatus() {
71:         return status;
72:     }
73: 
74:     public void setStatus(Status status) {
75:         this.status = status;
76:     }
77: 
78:     public UUID getCreatedBy() {
79:         return createdBy;
80:     }
81: 
82:     public void setCreatedBy(UUID createdBy) {
83:         this.createdBy = createdBy;
84:     }
85: 
86:     public Instant getCreatedOn() {
87:         return createdOn;
88:     }
89: 
90:     public void setCreatedOn(Instant createdOn) {
91:         this.createdOn = createdOn;
92:     }
93:

95:         return updatedBy;
96:     }

100:     }

104:     }
105: 
106:     public void setUpdatedOn(Instant updatedOn) {
107:         this.updatedOn = updatedOn;
108:     }
109: 
110:     @Override
111:     public boolean equals(Object other) {
112:         if (this == other) {
113:             return true;
114:         }
115:         if (!(other instanceof BaseEntity that)) {
116:             return false;
117:         }
118:         return id != null && id.equals(that.id);
119:     }
120: 
121:     @Override
122:     public int hashCode() {
123:         return Objects.hashCode(id);

1: package com.company.exam.common.exception;
2: 
3: import org.springframework.http.HttpStatus;
4: 
5: /**
6:  * Stable, machine-readable error categories and their default HTTP status mapping.
7:  *
8:  * <p>These are the minimum categories required by
9:  * {@code specs/09-P0-Foundation-V1.5.md §14} and {@code specs/06-Architecture-Freeze-V1.5.md §52}.
10:  * {@code PRECONDITION_FAILED} (HTTP 412) is included because the frozen API contract requires
11:  * precondition support for stale-write protection.
12:  */
13: public enum ErrorCode {
14: 
15:     /** Invalid request payload or field validation failure. */
16:     VALIDATION_ERROR(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST),
17: 
18:     /** Authentication required or failed. */
19:     AUTHENTICATION_ERROR(HttpStatus.UNAUTHORIZED),
20: 
21:     /** Authenticated but not permitted. */
22:     AUTHORIZATION_ERROR(HttpStatus.FORBIDDEN),
23: 
24:     /** Requested resource does not exist. */
25:     NOT_FOUND(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND),
26: 
27:     /** Conflicting state, duplicate, or optimistic-lock failure. */
28:     CONFLICT(HttpStatus.CONFLICT),
29: 
30:     /** Business-rule validation failure. */
31:     BUSINESS_ERROR(HttpStatus.UNPROCESSABLE_ENTITY),
32: 
33:     /** Operation not allowed in the current lifecycle state. */
34:     INVALID_STATE(HttpStatus.CONFLICT),
35: 
36:     /** Conditional request precondition failed (for example, stale write). */
37:     PRECONDITION_FAILED(HttpStatus.PRECONDITION_FAILED),
38: 
39:     /** Caller exceeded a rate limit. */
40:     RATE_LIMITED(HttpStatus.TOO_MANY_REQUESTS),
41: 
42:     /** HTTP method not supported for the resource. */
43:     METHOD_NOT_ALLOWED(HttpStatus.METHOD_NOT_ALLOWED),
44: 
45:     /** Request content type not supported for the resource. */
46:     UNSUPPORTED_MEDIA_TYPE(HttpStatus.UNSUPPORTED_MEDIA_TYPE),
47: 
48:     /** Unexpected server-side failure. */
49:     SYSTEM_ERROR(HttpStatus.INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR);
50: 
51:     /**
52:      * Maps an HTTP status to the closest documented error category, used when a framework
53:      * exception (an {@code ErrorResponse}) must be rendered in the standard envelope.
54:      */
55:     public static ErrorCode fromStatus(int statusCode) {
56:         return switch (statusCode) {
57:             case 400 -> VALIDATION_ERROR;
58:             case 401 -> AUTHENTICATION_ERROR;
59:             case 403 -> AUTHORIZATION_ERROR;
60:             case 404 -> NOT_FOUND;
61:             case 405 -> METHOD_NOT_ALLOWED;
62:             case 409 -> CONFLICT;
63:             case 412 -> PRECONDITION_FAILED;
64:             case 415 -> UNSUPPORTED_MEDIA_TYPE;
65:             case 422 -> BUSINESS_ERROR;
66:             case 429 -> RATE_LIMITED;
67:             default -> SYSTEM_ERROR;
68:         };
69:     }
70: 
71:     private final HttpStatus status;
72: 
73:     ErrorCode(HttpStatus status) {
74:         this.status = status;
75:     }
76: 
77:     public HttpStatus status() {
78:         return status;
79:     }
80: }

1: package com.company.exam.common.api;

3: import com.fasterxml.jackson.annotation.JsonInclude;
4: import com.fasterxml.jackson.annotation.JsonPropertyOrder;
5: import java.util.List;
6: 
7: /**
8:  * Standard success envelope returned by the API.
9:  *
10:  * <pre>
11:  * {
12:  *   "success": true,
13:  *   "message": "Request processed successfully",
14:  *   "data": {}
15:  * }
16:  * </pre>
17:  *
18:  * <p>Collection responses additionally carry a {@code pagination} object. The field is omitted
19:  * when {@code null}, so single-resource responses keep the documented shape.
20:  *
21:  * <p>See {@code specs/03-Technical-Design-Document-V1.5.md §19} and
22:  * {@code specs/09-P0-Foundation-V1.5.md §13}.
23:  */
24: @JsonInclude(JsonInclude.Include.NON_NULL)
25: @JsonPropertyOrder({"success", "message", "data", "pagination"})
26: public record ApiResponse<T>(boolean success, String message, T data, Pagination pagination) {
27: 
28:     public static final String DEFAULT_MESSAGE = "Request processed successfully";
29:     public static final String DEFAULT_COLLECTION_MESSAGE = "Records retrieved successfully";
30: 
31:     public static <T> ApiResponse<T> ok(T data) {
32:         return new ApiResponse<>(true, DEFAULT_MESSAGE, data, null);
33:     }
34: 
35:     public static <T> ApiResponse<T> ok(String message, T data) {
36:         return new ApiResponse<>(true, message, data, null);
37:     }
38: 
39:     public static <T> ApiResponse<List<T>> paged(PageResponse<T> page) {
40:         return paged(DEFAULT_COLLECTION_MESSAGE, page);
41:     }
42: 
43:     public static <T> ApiResponse<List<T>> paged(String message, PageResponse<T> page) {
44:         return new ApiResponse<>(true, message, page.content(), page.pagination());
45:     }
46: }
READbackend\src\main\java\com\company\exam\common\audit\CurrentAuditorProvider.java
Read 1/1 file, 44 lines
1: package com.company.exam.common.audit;
2: 
3: import java.util.Optional;
4: import java.util.UUID;
5: import org.springframework.data.domain.AuditorAware;
6: import org.springframework.security.core.Authentication;
7: import org.springframework.security.core.context.SecurityContextHolder;
8: import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
9: 
10: /**
11:  * Resolves the current auditor for JPA audit fields ({@code created_by}, {@code updated_by}).
12:  *
13:  * <p>P0 establishes the mechanism but does not implement the Identity domain. The JWT
14:  * authentication boundary sets the principal to the token subject; P1 Identity guarantees that
15:  * subject is the user's UUID. Until then, a non-UUID principal resolves to
16:  * {@link Optional#empty()} rather than a misleading attribution.
17:  *
18:  * <p>{@link #SYSTEM_ACTOR_ID} is the well-known system identity used by background and scheduled
19:  * operations ({@code specs/03-Technical-Design-Document-V1.5.md §31}); P1 wires background
20:  * operations to it explicitly.
21:  */
22: @Component
23: public class CurrentAuditorProvider implements AuditorAware<UUID> {
24: 
25:     /** Well-known system actor identity for background and scheduled operations. */
26:     public static final UUID SYSTEM_ACTOR_ID = UUID.fromString("00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000001");
27: 
28:     @Override
29:     public Optional<UUID> getCurrentAuditor() {
30:         Authentication authentication = SecurityContextHolder.getContext().getAuthentication();
31:         if (authentication == null || !authentication.isAuthenticated()) {
32:             return Optional.empty();
33:         }
34:         String principal = authentication.getName();
35:         if (principal == null) {
36:             return Optional.empty();
37:         }
38:         try {
39:             return Optional.of(UUID.fromString(principal));
40:         } catch (IllegalArgumentException notAUuid) {
41:             return Optional.empty();
42:         }
43:     }
44: }

66:             @Valid @RequestBody AssessmentDtos.ScheduleCreateRequest request,

1: package com.company.exam.content.api;
2: 
3: import java.math.BigDecimal;
4: import java.util.List;
5: import java.util.UUID;
6: 
7: /**
8:  * The explicit Content application contract consumed by Assessment
9:  * ({@code specs/13-P4-Assessment-V1.5.md §81, §82}).
10:  *
11:  * <p>Assessment verifies and selects published question versions through this contract rather than
12:  * reaching into Content repositories, preserving the modular-monolith boundary.
13:  */
14: public interface ContentCatalog {
15: 
16:     /** A summary of an exact published question version. */
17:     record PublishedQuestionVersion(UUID questionVersionId, UUID questionId, UUID languageId, String difficulty,
18:             BigDecimal marks, BigDecimal negativeMarks, String templateCode, boolean published) {
19:     }
20: 
21:     /** Selection criteria for rule-based authoring ({@code §45}). */
22:     record SelectionCriteria(String templateCode, UUID examId, UUID subjectId, UUID chapterId, UUID topicId,
23:             List<String> difficulties, List<UUID> tagIds, UUID languageId, Boolean publishedOnly,
24:             List<UUID> excludeQuestionIds, List<UUID> excludeQuestionVersionIds) {
25:     }
26: 
27:     /**
28:      * Returns the exact published question version, or throws a controlled error if it does not
29:      * exist or is not published.
30:      */
31:     PublishedQuestionVersion requirePublishedQuestionVersion(UUID questionVersionId);
32: 
33:     /** Returns the eligible published question versions matching the criteria. */
34:     List<PublishedQuestionVersion> findEligible(SelectionCriteria criteria);
35: 
36:     /** Canonical content plus its server-rendered HTML for preview ({@code 13-P4 §57}). */
37:     record PreviewContent(String contentFormat, String content, String renderedContent) {
38:     }
39: 
40:     PreviewContent previewContent(UUID questionVersionId);
41: }

1: package com.company.exam.content.service;

15: import com.company.exam.content.repository.QuestionVersionRepository;
16: import java.util.ArrayList;
17: import java.util.HashSet;
18: import java.util.List;
19: import java.util.Locale;
20: import java.util.Set;
21: import java.util.UUID;
22: import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
23: import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;
24: 
25: /** Default {@link ContentCatalog} ({@code specs/13-P4-Assessment-V1.5.md §82}). */
26: @Service
27: public class ContentCatalogService implements ContentCatalog {
28: 
29:     private final QuestionVersionRepository versionRepository;
30:     private final QuestionRepository questionRepository;
31:     private final QuestionTemplateRepository templateRepository;
32:     private final QuestionTagRepository questionTagRepository;
33:     private final com.company.exam.content.mapper.ContentMapper mapper;
34: 
35:     public ContentCatalogService(QuestionVersionRepository versionRepository, QuestionRepository questionRepository,
36:             QuestionTemplateRepository templateRepository, QuestionTagRepository questionTagRepository,
37:             com.company.exam.content.mapper.ContentMapper mapper) {
38:         this.versionRepository = versionRepository;
39:         this.questionRepository = questionRepository;
40:         this.templateRepository = templateRepository;
41:         this.questionTagRepository = questionTagRepository;
42:         this.mapper = mapper;
43:     }
44: 
45:     @Override
46:     @Transactional(readOnly = true)
47:     public PreviewContent previewContent(UUID questionVersionId) {
48:         QuestionVersion version = versionRepository.findById(questionVersionId)
49:                 .orElseThrow(() -> NotFoundException.of("Question version", questionVersionId));
50:         var rendered = mapper.toResponse(version);
51:         return new PreviewContent(rendered.contentFormat(), rendered.content(), rendered.renderedContent());
52:     }
53: 
54:     @Override
55:     @Transactional(readOnly = true)
56:     public PublishedQuestionVersion requirePublishedQuestionVersion(UUID questionVersionId) {
57:         QuestionVersion version = versionRepository.findById(questionVersionId)
58:                 .orElseThrow(() -> new BusinessException("Question version not found",
59:                         List.of(ApiError.of("questionVersionId", "QUESTION_VERSION_NOT_FOUND",
60:                                 "Question version does not exist"))));
61:         if (!version.isPublished()) {
62:             throw new BusinessException("Question version is not published",
63:                     List.of(ApiError.of("questionVersionId", "QUESTION_VERSION_NOT_PUBLISHED",
64:                             "Only published question versions may be used")));
65:         }
66:         return toSummary(version);
67:     }
68: 
69:     @Override
70:     @Transactional(readOnly = true)
71:     public List<PublishedQuestionVersion> findEligible(SelectionCriteria criteria) {
72:         Set<Difficulty> difficulties = parseDifficulties(criteria.difficulties());
73:         List<QuestionVersion> candidates = versionRepository.findEligibleVersions(
74:                 criteria.templateCode(),
75:                 criteria.examId(),
76:                 criteria.subjectId(),
77:                 criteria.chapterId(),
78:                 criteria.topicId(),
79:                 criteria.languageId(),
80:                 difficulties.isEmpty(),
81:                 difficulties.isEmpty() ? new ArrayList<>(List.of(Difficulty.EASY)) : new ArrayList<>(difficulties),
82:                 safe(criteria.excludeQuestionIds()),
83:                 safe(criteria.excludeQuestionVersionIds()));
84: 
85:         if (criteria.tagIds() == null || criteria.tagIds().isEmpty()) {
86:             return candidates.stream().map(this::toSummary).toList();
87:         }
88:         Set<UUID> wantedTags = new HashSet<>(criteria.tagIds());
89:         return candidates.stream()
90:                 .filter(version -> hasAnyTag(version.getQuestionId(), wantedTags))
91:                 .map(this::toSummary)
92:                 .toList();
93:     }
94: 
95:     private boolean hasAnyTag(UUID questionId, Set<UUID> tagIds) {
96:         return questionTagRepository.findByQuestionId(questionId).stream()
97:                 .filter(association -> association.getStatus() == com.company.exam.common.enums.Status.ACTIVE)
98:                 .anyMatch(association -> tagIds.contains(association.getTagId()));
99:     }
100: 
101:     private PublishedQuestionVersion toSummary(QuestionVersion version) {
102:         Question question = questionRepository.findById(version.getQuestionId()).orElse(null);
103:         String templateCode = question == null ? null
104:                 : templateRepository.findById(question.getTemplateId()).map(QuestionTemplate::getCode).orElse(null);
105:         return new PublishedQuestionVersion(version.getId(), version.getQuestionId(), version.getLanguageId(),
106:                 version.getDifficulty() == null ? null : version.getDifficulty().name(),
107:                 version.getMarks(), version.getNegativeMarks(), templateCode, version.isPublished());
108:     }
109: 
110:     private Set<Difficulty> parseDifficulties(List<String> values) {
111:         Set<Difficulty> result = new HashSet<>();
112:         if (values != null) {
113:             for (String value : values) {
114:                 if (value != null) {
115:                     try {
116:                         result.add(Difficulty.valueOf(value.trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT)));
117:                     } catch (IllegalArgumentException ignored) {
118:                         // Unknown difficulty is ignored rather than widening the search.
119:                     }
120:                 }
121:             }
122:         }
123:         return result;
124:     }
125: 
126:     private List<UUID> safe(List<UUID> values) {
127:         return values == null || values.isEmpty() ? List.of(PLACEHOLDER) : values;
128:     }
129: 
130:     private static final UUID PLACEHOLDER = UUID.fromString("00000000-0000-0000-0000-0000000000ff");
131: }

21: /**
22:  * Single global exception handler for the API.
23:  *
24:  * <p>Renders every controlled and unexpected failure into the standard error envelope
25:  * ({@code specs/09-P0-Foundation-V1.5.md §14, §103}). Unexpected exceptions are logged with
26:  * their correlation identifier; internal stack traces are never returned to clients.
27:  *
28:  * <p>See {@code specs/03-Technical-Design-Document-V1.5.md §22}.
29:  */
30: @RestControllerAdvice
31: public class GlobalExceptionHandler {
32: 
33:     private static final Logger log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(GlobalExceptionHandler.class);
34: 
35:     private static final String GENERIC_ERROR_MESSAGE = "An unexpected error occurred";
36:     private static final String VALIDATION_MESSAGE = "Validation failed";
37: 
38:     @ExceptionHandler(ApiException.class)
39:     public ResponseEntity<ApiErrorResponse> handleApiException(ApiException exception, HttpServletRequest request) {
40:         if (exception.getStatus().is5xxServerError()) {
41:             log.error("Request failed: errorCode={} path={}", exception.getErrorCode(), request.getRequestURI(),
42:                     exception);
43:         } else {
44:             log.debug("Request rejected: errorCode={} path={} message={}", exception.getErrorCode(),
45:                     request.getRequestURI(), exception.getMessage());
46:         }
47:         return build(exception.getStatus(), exception.getMessage(), exception.getErrors(), request);
48:     }
49: 
50:     @ExceptionHandler(MethodArgumentNotValidException.class)
51:     public ResponseEntity<ApiErrorResponse> handleMethodArgumentNotValid(MethodArgumentNotValidException exception,
52:             HttpServletRequest request) {
53:         List<ApiError> errors = exception.getBindingResult().getFieldErrors().stream()
54:                 .map(fieldError -> ApiError.of(
55:                         fieldError.getField(),
56:                         fieldError.getCode() == null ? "INVALID_VALUE" : fieldError.getCode(),
57:                         fieldError.getDefaultMessage() == null ? "Value is invalid" : fieldError.getDefaultMessage()))
58:                 .sorted(Comparator.comparing(ApiError::field))
59:                 .toList();
60:         return build(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST, VALIDATION_MESSAGE, errors, request);
61:     }
62: 
63:     @ExceptionHandler(ConstraintViolationException.class)
64:     public ResponseEntity<ApiErrorResponse> handleConstraintViolation(ConstraintViolationException exception,
65:             HttpServletRequest request) {
66:         List<ApiError> errors = exception.getConstraintViolations().stream()
67:                 .map(violation -> ApiError.of(
68:                         violation.getPropertyPath() == null ? null : violation.getPropertyPath().toString(),
69:                         "INVALID_VALUE",
70:                         violation.getMessage()))
71:                 .toList();
72:         return build(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST, VALIDATION_MESSAGE, errors, request);
73:     }
74: 
75:     @ExceptionHandler(HttpMessageNotReadableException.class)
76:     public ResponseEntity<ApiErrorResponse> handleUnreadableBody(HttpMessageNotReadableException exception,
77:             HttpServletRequest request) {
78:         log.debug("Malformed request body on path={}", request.getRequestURI());
79:         return build(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST, "Malformed request body", null, request);
80:     }
81: 
82:     /**
83:      * A path or query parameter could not be converted to its target type (for example, a malformed
84:      * UUID) is a client error, not an internal failure.
85:      */
86:     @ExceptionHandler(org.springframework.web.method.annotation.MethodArgumentTypeMismatchException.class)
87:     public ResponseEntity<ApiErrorResponse> handleTypeMismatch(
88:             org.springframework.web.method.annotation.MethodArgumentTypeMismatchException exception,
89:             HttpServletRequest request) {
90:         log.debug("Invalid value for parameter '{}' on path={}", exception.getName(), request.getRequestURI());
91:         return build(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST, "Invalid value for parameter '" + exception.getName() + "'", null,
92:                 request);
93:     }
94: 
95:     @ExceptionHandler(NoResourceFoundException.class)
96:     public ResponseEntity<ApiErrorResponse> handleNoResource(NoResourceFoundException exception,
97:             HttpServletRequest request) {
98:         return build(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "Resource not found", null, request);
99:     }
100: 
101:     /**
102:      * Method-security denials ({@code @PreAuthorize}) are thrown during handler invocation and
103:      * would otherwise be absorbed by the catch-all below. Rendering them here keeps the security
104:      * response contract identical to the filter-level handlers.
105:      */
106:     @ExceptionHandler(org.springframework.security.access.AccessDeniedException.class)
107:     public ResponseEntity<ApiErrorResponse> handleAccessDenied(
108:             org.springframework.security.access.AccessDeniedException exception, HttpServletRequest request) {
109:         log.debug("Access denied on path={}", request.getRequestURI());
110:         return build(HttpStatus.FORBIDDEN, "Access denied", null, request);
111:     }
112: 
113:     @ExceptionHandler(org.springframework.security.core.AuthenticationException.class)
114:     public ResponseEntity<ApiErrorResponse> handleAuthentication(
115:             org.springframework.security.core.AuthenticationException exception, HttpServletRequest request) {
116:         log.debug("Authentication failure on path={}", request.getRequestURI());
117:         return build(HttpStatus.UNAUTHORIZED, "Authentication required", null, request);
118:     }
119: 
120:     /**
121:      * Catch-all for anything not handled above.
122:      *
123:      * <p>Framework exceptions that already carry an HTTP status (for example, method not allowed
124:      * or unsupported media type) keep that status so HTTP semantics remain correct. Everything
125:      * else becomes a generic 500 without leaking internals.
126:      */
127:     @ExceptionHandler(Exception.class)
128:     public ResponseEntity<ApiErrorResponse> handleUnexpected(Exception exception, HttpServletRequest request) {
129:         if (exception instanceof ErrorResponse errorResponse) {
130:             HttpStatus status = HttpStatus.resolve(errorResponse.getStatusCode().value());
131:             if (status != null && status.is4xxClientError()) {
132:                 log.debug("Framework client error: status={} path={}", status.value(), request.getRequestURI());
133:                 return build(status, status.getReasonPhrase(), null, request);
134:             }
135:         }
136:         log.error("Unhandled exception on path={}", request.getRequestURI(), exception);
137:         return build(HttpStatus.INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR, GENERIC_ERROR_MESSAGE, null, request);
138:     }
139: 
140:     private ResponseEntity<ApiErrorResponse> build(HttpStatus status, String message, List<ApiError> errors,
141:             HttpServletRequest request) {
142:         ApiErrorResponse body = ApiErrorResponse.of(message, errors, request.getRequestURI(),
143:                 RequestContext.getRequestId());
144:         return ResponseEntity.status(status).body(body);
145:     }
146: }

8: /**
9:  * Default {@link DeliveryPolicyCatalog} ({@code specs/13-P4-Assessment-V1.5.md §83.1}).
10:  *
11:  * <p>Reads the Delivery-owned {@code delivery_policy} table for existence. The table is created in
12:  * migration V8 (see {@code docs/known-gaps.md} GAP-040) because the frozen
13:  * {@code assessment_version.delivery_policy_id} foreign key requires it.
14:  */
15: @Service
16: public class DeliveryPolicyCatalogService implements DeliveryPolicyCatalog {
17: 
18:     private final JdbcTemplate jdbcTemplate;
19: 
20:     public DeliveryPolicyCatalogService(JdbcTemplate jdbcTemplate) {
21:         this.jdbcTemplate = jdbcTemplate;
22:     }
23: 
24:     @Override
25:     public boolean exists(UUID deliveryPolicyId) {
26:         if (deliveryPolicyId == null) {
27:             return false;
28:         }
29:         Integer count = jdbcTemplate.queryForObject(
30:                 "select count(*) from delivery_policy where id = ? and status = 'ACTIVE'",
31:                 Integer.class, deliveryPolicyId);
32:         return count != null && count > 0;
33:     }
34: }

11: /**
12:  * Builds JWTs for tests using the same issuer and secret as the {@code test} profile.
13:  *
14:  * <p>P0 does not issue tokens; the security smoke tests need a validly signed token to prove that
15:  * the JWT boundary authenticates a caller and that an authenticated caller without the required
16:  * authority receives 403.
17:  */
18: public final class TestTokens {
19: 
20:     public static final String ISSUER = "exam-platform";
21:     public static final String SECRET = "test-only-secret-value-at-least-32-characters-long";
22: 
23:     private TestTokens() {
24:     }
25: 
26:     public static String forSubject(String subject) {
27:         return Jwts.builder()
28:                 .subject(subject)
29:                 .issuer(ISSUER)
30:                 .issuedAt(new Date())
31:                 .expiration(Date.from(Instant.now().plus(10, ChronoUnit.MINUTES)))
32:                 .signWith(secretKey(), Jwts.SIG.HS256)
33:                 .compact();
34:     }
35: 
36:     public static String forSubjectWithIssuer(String subject, String issuer) {
37:         return Jwts.builder()
38:                 .subject(subject)
39:                 .issuer(issuer)
40:                 .issuedAt(new Date())
41:                 .expiration(Date.from(Instant.now().plus(10, ChronoUnit.MINUTES)))
42:                 .signWith(secretKey(), Jwts.SIG.HS256)
43:                 .compact();
44:     }
45: 
46:     public static String expired(String subject) {
47:         return Jwts.builder()
48:                 .subject(subject)
49:                 .issuer(ISSUER)
50:                 .issuedAt(Date.from(Instant.now().minus(2, ChronoUnit.HOURS)))
51:                 .expiration(Date.from(Instant.now().minus(1, ChronoUnit.HOURS)))
52:                 .signWith(secretKey(), Jwts.SIG.HS256)
53:                 .compact();
54:     }
55: 
56:     public static SecretKey secretKey() {
57:         return Keys.hmacShaKeyFor(SECRET.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
58:     }
59: }

Key correctness points:
1. Start Exam does zero snapshot inserts: StartAssessmentService only does conditional update on student_attempt + session create/activate. Test asserts attempt_question count unchanged.

I'll create a `StaleResponseException extends ApiException` with PRECONDITION_FAILED + ApiError("response","STALE_RESPONSE","...").
3. Recovery returns same snapshot: SessionRecoveryService returns runtime context (attemptId, sessionId, questions count, snapshot status, deadline) without inserting.
4. Start burst: concurrency test with N threads calling start for distinct attempts; assert all succeed and no snapshot inserts.

9:  *
10:  * <p>Every {@code ApiException} carries an {@link ErrorCode}, which determines the HTTP status.
11:  * The {@link GlobalExceptionHandler} renders it as the standard error envelope.
12:  *
13:  * <p>Messages passed here are safe to return to clients: they must never contain stack traces,
14:  * SQL, credentials, tokens, or internal filesystem paths.
15:  */
16: public class ApiException extends RuntimeException {
17: 
18:     private final ErrorCode errorCode;
19:     private final transient List<ApiError> errors;
20: 
21:     public ApiException(ErrorCode errorCode, String message) {
22:         this(errorCode, message, List.of(), null);
23:     }
24: 
25:     public ApiException(ErrorCode errorCode, String message, List<ApiError> errors) {
26:         this(errorCode, message, errors, null);
27:     }
28: 
29:     public ApiException(ErrorCode errorCode, String message, Throwable cause) {
30:         this(errorCode, message, List.of(), cause);
31:     }
32: 
33:     public ApiException(ErrorCode errorCode, String message, List<ApiError> errors, Throwable cause) {
34:         super(message, cause);
35:         this.errorCode = errorCode;
36:         this.errors = errors == null ? List.of() : List.copyOf(errors);
37:     }
38: 
39:     public ErrorCode getErrorCode() {
40:         return errorCode;
41:     }
42: 
43:     public HttpStatus getStatus() {
44:         return errorCode.status();
45:     }
46: 
47:     public List<ApiError> getErrors() {
48:         return errors;
49:     }
50: }

10: public record ApiError(String field, String code, String message) {

15:

DeclarativeExamEngine:
```java
@Component
public class DeclarativeExamEngine implements ExamEngine {
  public EngineType type() { return EngineType.CUSTOM; }
  public DeliveryConfiguration resolveConfiguration(ExamEngineContext ctx) {
    DeliveryPolicyAttributes p = ctx.deliveryPolicyAttributes() == null ? defaults : ctx...
    boolean practice = "PRACTICE".equals(ctx.mode());
    String navMode = practice ? "FREE" : (p.allowBackNavigation() ? "FREE" : "SEQUENTIAL");
    Integer durationSeconds = ctx.durationMinutes() == null ? null : ctx.durationMinutes()*60;
    boolean enforced = !practice && durationSeconds != null;
    return new DeliveryConfiguration(
       new Capabilities(practice, p.allowClearResponse(), p.allowMarkForReview(), practice, practice),
       new NavigationPolicy(navMode, p.allowBackNavigation(), p.allowSectionSwitch(), p.allowQuestionReview()),
       new TimerPolicy("GLOBAL", durationSeconds, enforced),
       new SubmissionPolicy(p.autoSubmitOnExpiry(), true),
       new ValidationPolicy(true, 10000),
       new PresentationPolicy(true, true, true, MarkdownLatexRenderer.CONTENT_FORMAT? ...));
  }
}
```

@Component
public class ExamEngineRegistry {

  private final ExamEngine defaultEngine;
  public ExamEngineRegistry(List<ExamEngine> all) { ... build map by type(); defaultEngine = pick CUSTOM or first }
  public ExamEngine resolve(EngineType type) { return engines.getOrDefault(type, defaultEngine); }
}

Let me write DeliveryPolicyService: given deliveryPolicyId → attributes.
```java
@Service
public class DeliveryPolicyService {
  private final DeliveryPolicyRepository repo; private final DeliveryPolicyParser parser;
  public DeliveryPolicyAttributes resolve(UUID policyId) {
    if (policyId == null) return defaults;
    return repo.findById(policyId).map(p -> parser.parse(p.getConfiguration())).orElseGet(defaults);
  }
}
```

RuntimeConfigurationService:
```java
@Service
public class RuntimeConfigurationService {
  private final AssessmentDefinitionCatalog assessment; private final ExamEngineRegistry registry; private final DeliveryPolicyService policyService;
  public DeliveryConfiguration resolve(ScheduleView schedule, PublishedAssessmentVersion version, List<String> sectionOrder) {
    var engineType = version.engineType()==null? EngineType.CUSTOM : EngineType.valueOf(version.engineType());
    var ctx = new ExamEngineContext(engineType, version.id(), schedule.mode().name(), version.durationMinutes(), sectionOrder, policyService.resolve(version.deliveryPolicyId()));
    return registry.resolve(engineType).resolveConfiguration(ctx);
  }
}
```

```java
@Service
public class AttemptEligibilityService {
  private final StudentAttemptRepository attempts;
  private final AssessmentDefinitionCatalog assessment;

  @Transactional(readOnly=true)
  public StudentAttempt requireStartable(UUID scheduleId, UUID userId) {
    ScheduleView schedule = assessment.requireSchedule(scheduleId);   // 404 if missing
    Instant now = Instant.now();
    if (!"ACTIVE".equals(schedule.status())) throw new InvalidStateException("Schedule is not active");
    if (now.isBefore(schedule.startTime()) || now.isAfter(schedule.endTime())) throw new InvalidStateException("Schedule window is not open");
    List<StudentAttempt> list = attempts.findByAssessmentScheduleIdAndSnapshotStatus(scheduleId, SnapshotStatus.READY) filtered by userId & NOT_STARTED;
    ...
  }
}

Let me design StartAssessmentService to:
1. resolve schedule (404/409 window/status).
2. find candidate attempts for user+schedule.
3. if none → 409 PREPARATION_NOT_READY.
4. choose: attempt with status IN_PROGRESS (existing) OR snapshotStatus READY & NOT_STARTED.
   - if none READY → 409 PREPARATION_NOT_READY.
5. if existing IN_PROGRESS: find active session; if exists return StartResponse (idempotent). else create session and return.
6. else (READY+NOT_STARTED): perform conditional markStarted; if 1 row affected → we own start; create/activate session; return. If 0 rows (concurrent winner) → re-read attempt; if IN_PROGRESS return established context (idempotent).

19:  * The focused authorization decision service ({@code specs/10-P1-Identity-V1.5.md §69}).
20:  *
21:  * <p>It answers only permission questions. It does not own question validation, assessment rules,
22:  * payment logic, or evaluation. Effective permissions are resolved from PostgreSQL (a single join
23:  * query) and cached in Redis with a short TTL. A cache outage resolves from PostgreSQL and never
24:  * grants access ({@code §71-§74, §149}).
25:  */

44:         if (userId == null || permissionCode == null) {
45:             return false;
46:         }
47:         return effectivePermissions(userId).contains(permissionCode);
48:     }
49: 
50:     /** SpEL-friendly check used by {@code @PreAuthorize("@authorizationService.hasPermission(authentication, 'X')")}. */
51:     public boolean hasPermission(Authentication authentication, String permissionCode) {
52:         return currentUserId(authentication).map(userId -> hasPermission(userId, permissionCode)).orElse(false);
53:     }
54: 
55:     /** Throws 403 when the user does not hold the permission. */
56:     public void requirePermission(UUID userId, String permissionCode) {
57:         if (!hasPermission(userId, permissionCode)) {
58:             throw new AuthorizationException("Access denied");
59:         }
60:     }
61: 
62:     /**
63:      * Effective, distinct permission codes for a user. PostgreSQL is authoritative; Redis is a
64:      * short-lived optimization only.
65:      */
66:     public List<String> effectivePermissions(UUID userId) {
67:         String key = KEY_PREFIX + userId;
68:         Optional<CachedPermissions> cached = cacheService.get(key, CachedPermissions.class);
69:         if (cached.isPresent()) {
70:             return cached.get().permissionCodes();
71:         }
72:         List<String> resolved = permissionRepository.findEffectivePermissionCodes(userId);
73:         cacheService.put(key, new CachedPermissions(resolved), CACHE_TTL);
74:         return resolved;
75:     }
76: 
77:     public Optional<UUID> currentUserId(Authentication authentication) {
78:         if (authentication == null || !authentication.isAuthenticated()) {
79:             return Optional.empty();
80:         }
81:         Object principal = authentication.getPrincipal();
82:         if (principal instanceof IdentityPrincipal identityPrincipal) {
83:             return Optional.of(identityPrincipal.userId());
84:         }
85:         return Optional.empty();
86:     }
87: 
88:     public Optional<IdentityPrincipal> currentPrincipal(Authentication authentication) {
89:         if (authentication != null && authentication.getPrincipal() instanceof IdentityPrincipal principal) {
90:             return Optional.of(principal);
91:         }
92:         return Optional.empty();
93:     }
94: 
95:     /** Evicts the cached permissions for a user. Called after a permission-affecting commit. */
96:     public void evict(UUID userId) {
97:         if (userId != null) {
98:             cacheService.evict(KEY_PREFIX + userId);
99:         }
100:     }
101: 
102:     /**
103:      * Evicts only after the surrounding transaction commits, so a failed transaction never
104:      * invalidates a good cache entry ({@code §72}).
105:      */
106:     public void evictAfterCommit(UUID userId) {
107:         if (userId == null) {
108:             return;
109:         }
110:         if (TransactionSynchronizationManager.isSynchronizationActive()) {
111:             TransactionSynchronizationManager.registerSynchronization(new TransactionSynchronization() {
112:                 @Override
113:                 public void afterCommit() {
114:                     evict(userId);
115:                 }
116:             });
117:         } else {
118:             log.debug("No active transaction; evicting authorization cache immediately for userId={}", userId);
119:             evict(userId);
120:         }
121:     }
122: 
123:     /** Cache envelope so {@link CacheService} can deserialize a typed value. */
124:     public record CachedPermissions(List<String> permissionCodes) {
125:     }
126: }

Implementation:
```java
@Service
public class CandidateDirectoryService implements CandidateDirectory {
  private final RoleRepository roleRepository; private final UserRoleRepository userRoleRepository; private final UserRepository userRepository;
  public List<UUID> activeCandidateUserIds() {
    Optional<Role> student = roleRepository.findByCode("STUDENT");
    if (student.isEmpty()) return List.of();
    List<UUID> ids = userRoleRepository.findByRoleIdAndStatus(student.get().getId(), Status.ACTIVE).stream().map(UserRole::getUserId).distinct().toList();
    // filter to ACTIVE users
    return ids.stream().filter(id -> userRepository.findByIdAndStatus(id, Status.ACTIVE).isPresent()).toList();
  }
}

AttemptEligibilityService:
```java
@Service
public class AttemptEligibilityService {
  private final StudentAttemptRepository attempts;
  private final AssessmentDefinitionCatalog assessment;
  public ScheduleView requireOpenSchedule(UUID scheduleId) {
    ScheduleView s = assessment.requireSchedule(scheduleId);
    Instant now = Instant.now();
    if (!"ACTIVE".equals(s.status())) throw new InvalidStateException("Schedule is not active");
    if (s.startTime()!=null && now.isBefore(s.startTime())) throw new InvalidStateException("Assessment has not started");
    if (s.endTime()!=null && now.isAfter(s.endTime())) throw new InvalidStateException("Assessment window has closed");
    return s;
  }
  public StudentAttempt requireOwned(UUID attemptId, UUID userId) {
    return attempts.findByIdAndUserId(attemptId, userId).orElseThrow(() -> NotFoundException.of("Attempt", attemptId));
  }
  public List<StudentAttempt> candidates(UUID scheduleId, UUID userId) {
    return attempts.findByAssessmentScheduleIdAndUserId(scheduleId, userId);
  }
}
```

Let me write StartAssessmentService:
```java
@Service
public class StartAssessmentService {
  repos: StudentAttemptRepository, AssessmentDefinitionCatalog, RuntimeConfigurationService, AssessmentSessionService, AttemptEligibilityService

  @Transactional
  public DeliveryDtos.StartResponse start(UUID scheduleId, UUID userId) {
    ScheduleView schedule = eligibility.requireOpenSchedule(scheduleId);
    PublishedAssessmentVersion version = assessment.requirePublishedVersion(schedule.assessmentVersionId());

    List<StudentAttempt> candidates = attempts.findByAssessmentScheduleIdAndUserId(scheduleId, userId);
    if (candidates.isEmpty()) throw new PreparationNotReadyException();

    // Idempotent: an already-started attempt returns its established session.
    StudentAttempt started = candidates.stream().filter(a -> a.getAttemptStatus()==IN_PROGRESS || a.getAttemptStatus()==SUBMITTED || a.getAttemptStatus()==EVALUATED).findFirst().orElse(null);
    if (started != null && started.getAttemptStatus() != NOT_STARTED) {
       // returns established context
       return context(started, schedule, version, userId);
    }

    StudentAttempt ready = candidates.stream().filter(a -> a.getSnapshotStatus()==READY && a.getAttemptStatus()==NOT_STARTED).findFirst().orElse(null);
    if (ready == null) throw new PreparationNotReadyException();

    Instant now = Instant.now();
    int updated = attempts.markStarted(ready.getId(), NOT_STARTED, IN_PROGRESS, READY, userId, now);
    StudentAttempt attempt;
    if (updated == 1) { attempt = attempts.findById(ready.getId()).orElseThrow(); }
    else {
       attempt = attempts.findById(ready.getId()).orElseThrow();
       if (attempt.getAttemptStatus()==NOT_STARTED) throw new InvalidStateException("Attempt could not be started");
    }
    return context(attempt, schedule, version, userId);
  }
  ...
}
```

```java
private StartResponse context(StudentAttempt a, ScheduleView s, PublishedAssessmentVersion v, UUID userId) {
  Instant now = Instant.now();
  var sections = questionRepo.findDistinctSectionNames(a.getId());
  DeliveryConfiguration config = runtimeConfig.resolve(s, v, sections);
  AssessmentSession session = sessionService.activateOrCreate(a, userId, now);
  return new StartResponse(session.getId(), a.getId(), a.getAssessmentVersionId(), a.getAttemptMode().name(), a.getStartedOn(), now, timer(a, config), config);
}

AssessmentSessionService:
```java
@Service
public class AssessmentSessionService {
  repos: AssessmentSessionRepository, StudentAttemptRepository, AssessmentDefinitionCatalog, RuntimeConfigurationService

  @Transactional
  public AssessmentSession activateOrCreate(StudentAttempt attempt, UUID userId, Instant now) {
    return sessions.findFirstByAttemptIdAndSessionStatusOrderByStartedOnDesc(attempt.getId(), ACTIVE)
      .orElseGet(() -> { AssessmentSession s = new AssessmentSession(); s.setUserId(userId); s.setAssessmentScheduleId(attempt.getAssessmentScheduleId()); s.setAttemptId(attempt.getId()); s.setSessionStatus(ACTIVE); s.setStartedOn(now); s.setLastActivityOn(now); return sessions.save(s); });
  }

  findActive(userId) → list; if empty throw NotFoundException("Active session")
  get(sessionId,userId) → requireOwned
  recover(sessionId,userId) → requireOwned; verify ACTIVE; verify attempt state; re-resolve config; update lastActivityOn; return SessionResponse (no snapshot rebuild)
  end(sessionId,userId) → requireOwner or DELIVERY_OPERATE; set sessionStatus=LOGGED_OUT, endedOn=now (does not submit attempt)
  touch(sessionId, now) → update lastActivityOn
}
```

Let me write SnapshotPreparationService carefully.

```java
@Service
public class SnapshotPreparationService {
  private final StudentAttemptRepository attempts;
  private final AttemptQuestionRepository questions;
  private final AttemptQuestionOptionRepository options;
  private final AssessmentDefinitionCatalog assessment;
  private final ContentCatalog content;
  private final CandidateDirectory candidates;

  @Transactional
  public int createPendingAttemptsForSchedule(UUID scheduleId, UUID actorId) {
    ScheduleView schedule = assessment.requireSchedule(scheduleId);
    assessment.requirePublishedVersion(schedule.assessmentVersionId());  // ensure published
    int created = 0;
    for (UUID userId : candidates.activeCandidateUserIds()) {
      if (attempts.findByAssessmentScheduleIdAndUserId(scheduleId, userId).isEmpty()) {
        StudentAttempt a = new StudentAttempt();
        a.setUserId(userId); a.setAssessmentVersionId(schedule.assessmentVersionId());
        a.setAssessmentScheduleId(scheduleId); a.setAttemptMode(schedule.mode());
        a.setAttemptStatus(NOT_STARTED); a.setSnapshotStatus(PENDING);
        attempts.save(a); created++;
      }
    }
    return created;
  }

  @Transactional
  public boolean prepare(UUID attemptId) {
    Instant now = Instant.now();
    int claimed = attempts.claimForPreparation(attemptId, PENDING, PREPARING, now);
    if (claimed != 1) return false;   // not owner
    StudentAttempt attempt = attempts.findById(attemptId).orElseThrow();
    try {
      materialize(attempt, now);
      attempts.markSnapshotReady(attemptId, PREPARING, READY, Instant.now());
      return true;
    } catch (RuntimeException e) {
      log.error(...);
      attempts.markSnapshotFailed(attemptId, PREPARING, FAILED, Instant.now());
      return false;
    }
  }

  private void materialize(StudentAttempt attempt, Instant now) {
    PublishedAssessmentVersion version = assessment.requirePublishedVersion(attempt.getAssessmentVersionId());
    List<AssessmentQuestionView> definition = assessment.questions(version.id());
    if (definition.isEmpty()) throw new IllegalStateException("Assessment has no questions");
    int order = 0;
    for (AssessmentQuestionView q : definition) {
      RuntimeQuestion content = content.runtimeQuestion(q.questionVersionId());
      if (content.options().isEmpty()) throw new IllegalStateException("Question has no options");
      AttemptQuestion aq = new AttemptQuestion();
      aq.setAttemptId(attempt.getId()); aq.setQuestionVersionId(q.questionVersionId());
      aq.setDisplayOrder(q.displayOrder()==null?++order:q.displayOrder());
      aq.setSectionName(q.sectionName()); aq.setMarks(q.marks()); aq.setNegativeMarks(q.negativeMarks());
      aq.setCreatedOn(now);
      AttemptQuestion saved = questions.save(aq);
      int optOrder = 0;
      for (RuntimeOption o : content.options()) {
        AttemptQuestionOption aqo = new AttemptQuestionOption();
        aqo.setAttemptQuestionId(saved.getId()); aqo.setQuestionOptionId(o.optionId());
        aqo.setDisplayOrder(o.displayOrder()==null?++optOrder:o.displayOrder());
        options.save(aqo);
      }
      // integrity: expected options
      long materialized = options.countByAttemptQuestionId(saved.getId());
      if (materialized != content.options().size()) throw new IllegalStateException("Option snapshot incomplete");
    }
    long materializedQuestions = questions.countByAttemptId(attempt.getId());
    if (materializedQuestions != definition.size()) throw new IllegalStateException("Question snapshot incomplete");
  }

  @Transactional
  public int preparePendingBatch(int limit) { ... }  // used by worker

  @Transactional
  public int retryFailed(UUID scheduleId) { ... }  // FAILED -> PENDING then prepare
}
```

Note: I must be careful about transactional boundaries: `prepare(attemptId)` is @Transactional and calls content.runtimeQuestion (content service @Transactional readOnly) — nested in same tx, fine (propagation REQUIRED joins).

Also bulk @Modifying within the same transaction as inserts: the claim update clears the persistence context; subsequent saves flush at commit. markSnapshotReady is a bulk update that requires the INSERTs to have been flushed already. Since we `questions.save()` then bulk update at the end, Hibernate may not flush inserts before the bulk update (bulk updates bypass persistence context and don't auto-flush unless flushAutomatically=true). I set flushAutomatically=true on markSnapshotReady, so Hibernate flushes pending inserts first. 

But there's a subtlety: bulk update + clearAutomatically=true will clear the context after; but we're done. Also the reads (countByAttemptQuestionId) are queries which trigger auto-flush. Good.

Also: `options.countByAttemptQuestionId(saved.getId())` — saved.getId() available after save (UUID assigned by Hibernate on persist). Good.

}

public DeliveryDtos.SessionResponse end(UUID sessionId, UUID userId, boolean operate) {
  AssessmentSession session = operate ? sessions.findById(sessionId).orElseThrow(...) : requireOwned(sessionId,userId);
  if (session.getSessionStatus()==ACTIVE) { session.setSessionStatus(LOGGED_OUT); session.setEndedOn(now); sessions.save(session); }
  // does NOT submit the attempt
  return describe(...);
}

private DeliveryDtos.SessionResponse describe(AssessmentSession session) {
  StudentAttempt attempt = attempts.findById(session.getAttemptId()).orElseThrow(...);
  ScheduleView schedule = assessment.requireSchedule(session.getAssessmentScheduleId());
  PublishedAssessmentVersion version = assessment.requirePublishedVersion(attempt.getAssessmentVersionId());
  List<String> sections = questions.findDistinctSectionNames(attempt.getId());
  DeliveryConfiguration config = runtimeConfig.resolve(schedule, version, sections);
  return new SessionResponse(session.getId(), session.getAttemptId(), session.getAssessmentScheduleId(), session.getSessionStatus().name(), session.getStartedOn(), session.getLastActivityOn(), session.getEndedOn(), Instant.now(), timer(attempt, config));
}
```

Let me create `delivery/application/DeliveryTimers.java`:
```java
final class DeliveryTimers {
  static DeliveryDtos.TimerView timer(StudentAttempt attempt, DeliveryConfiguration config) {
    Integer secs = config.timer().durationSeconds();
    Instant started = attempt.getStartedOn();
    Instant deadline = (started != null && secs != null) ? started.plusSeconds(secs) : null;
    return new DeliveryDtos.TimerView(config.timer().mode(), secs, deadline);
  }
}
```

Careful: it calls attemptRepository.markStarted (bulk, clearAutomatically) then re-reads and calls AssessmentSessionService.activateOrCreate (which is @Transactional on another bean — joining the outer tx). Since StartAssessmentService.start is @Transactional, activateOrCreate joins.

Order: 
1. requireOpenSchedule
2. requirePublishedVersion
3. candidates
4. if none → PreparationNotReadyException
5. find an attempt that is already IN_PROGRESS/SUBMITTED/EVALUATED → return established context (idempotent).
   Actually if SUBMITTED, starting again should probably return context or invalid state. I'll return context for IN_PROGRESS; for SUBMITTED/EVALUATED throw InvalidStateException("Attempt already submitted"). Simpler and deterministic.
6. find READY+NOT_STARTED candidate; if none → PreparationNotReadyException.
7. markStarted; re-read attempt.
   - if attempt.attemptStatus == IN_PROGRESS → ok
   - else → concurrency loser; re-read and if IN_PROGRESS ok else InvalidState.
8. build context.

Note: after markStarted bulk update with clearAutomatically, re-read gives IN_PROGRESS and startedOn set.

Build context:
```
private StartResponse context(StudentAttempt attempt, ScheduleView schedule, PublishedAssessmentVersion version, UUID userId) {
  Instant now = Instant.now();
  List<String> sections = questionRepository.findDistinctSectionNames(attempt.getId());
  DeliveryConfiguration config = runtimeConfigurationService.resolve(schedule, version, sections);
  AssessmentSession session = sessionService.activateOrCreate(attempt, userId, now);
  return new StartResponse(session.getId(), attempt.getId(), attempt.getAssessmentVersionId(), attempt.getAttemptMode().name(), attempt.getStartedOn(), now, DeliveryTimers.timer(attempt, config), config);
}
```

ResponseSaveService:
```java
@Service
public class ResponseSaveService {
  private final StudentAttemptRepository attempts; private final AttemptQuestionRepository questions; private final StudentResponseRepository responses; private final AssessmentSessionService sessions; private final AttemptEligibilityService eligibility; private final int maxResponseLength = 10000;

  @Transactional(readOnly=true)
  public DeliveryDtos.ResponseView get(attemptId, questionVersionId, userId) {
    StudentAttempt attempt = eligibility.requireOwned(attemptId, userId);
    requireAssigned(attemptId, questionVersionId);
    StudentResponse response = responses.findByAttemptIdAndQuestionVersionId(attemptId, questionVersionId)
        .orElseThrow(() -> NotFoundException("Response not found"));
    return toView(response, etag(response));
  }

  @Transactional
  public DeliveryDtos.ResponseView save(attemptId, questionVersionId, request, ifMatch, userId) {
    StudentAttempt attempt = eligibility.requireOwned(attemptId, userId);
    if (attempt.getAttemptStatus() != IN_PROGRESS) throw new InvalidStateException("Attempt is not in progress");
    requireAssigned(attemptId, questionVersionId);
    validate(request);
    StudentResponse existing = responses.findByAttemptIdAndQuestionVersionId(attemptId, questionVersionId).orElse(null);
    checkPrecondition(ifMatch, existing);
    StudentResponse target = existing != null ? existing : newResponse(attemptId, questionVersionId);
    target.setResponse(normalize(request.response()));
    target.setTimeSpentSeconds(request.timeSpentSeconds());
    target.setMarkedForReview(request.markedForReview());
    target.setVisited(request.visited());
    StudentResponse saved = responses.saveAndFlush(target);
    sessions.touch(attemptId, Instant.now());
    return toView(saved, etag(saved));
  }
}
```

checkPrecondition:
```java
if (ifMatch == null) return;         // first save / accept
if (ifMatch.equals("*")) { if (existing == null) throw new StaleResponseException(); return; }
if (existing == null) throw new StaleResponseException();
if (!ifMatch.equals(etag(existing))) throw new StaleResponseException();
```

etag: `"resp-" + updatedOn.toEpochMilli() + "-" + crc32(response)`. Format from spec: `"resp-{updatedOnMillis}-{crc32OfResponse}"`. Include quotes? The spec shows ETag value with quotes in header. I'll produce the raw value without quotes: `resp-<millis>-<crc>` and let HTTP layer add quotes? Actually HTTP ETag requires quotes. The spec string `"resp-..."` includes quotes as literal characters? In HTTP header, ETag: "resp-123-456" — the quotes ARE part of the header value syntax. So the etag token is `resp-123-456` and header is `"resp-123-456"`. For If-Match the client sends `"resp-123-456"` including quotes. 

To be safe and consistent, I'll treat the ETag value as including the quotes: `"resp-123-456"` (with quotes). Then If-Match header equals that string. Let me define etag() returns `"resp-" + millis + "-" + crc + "\""` i.e. with surrounding quotes? Simpler: return quoted string `"\"" + "resp-" + millis + "-" + crc + "\""`. And comparisons strip quotes on both sides to be lenient.

I'll normalize: strip surrounding quotes from If-Match before comparing to the unquoted token. And return the unquoted token in ResponseView.etag; the controller sets the ETag header as `"\"" + token + "\""`.

Hmm, the test will do: save → get etag from response body (or header) → send If-Match. I'll expose the etag in the JSON body (ResponseView.etag) as the raw token without quotes, and the controller writes the header as `"<token>"`. For If-Match I'll strip quotes. That's robust.

Actually to reduce ambiguity, I'll make the etag token itself unquoted and in the controller set header value = '"' + token + '"'. And parse If-Match by trimming quotes.

Let me implement ResponseSaveService.etag(response) returning unquoted token.

crc32:
```java
private long crc32(String value) {
  CRC32 crc = new CRC32();
  crc.update((value == null ? "" : value).getBytes(UTF_8));
  return crc.getValue();
}
```

etag needs updatedOn non-null. saveAndFlush sets it via auditing. For existing rows, updatedOn updated on flush because entity modified. Good. But auditing's @LastModifiedDate sets updatedOn = now on flush — yes.

For the "increment updated_on" requirement: modifying the entity marks it dirty; @LastModifiedDate updates on flush. But if the values are identical (same response, same flags), Hibernate may consider it not dirty and skip the UPDATE → updatedOn unchanged → new ETag equals old ETag → a save with matching If-Match returns same etag. Spec says "increment updated_on". To guarantee a new ETag on every accepted save, I can explicitly bump updatedOn. But updatedOn is managed by auditing (@LastModifiedDate). Setting it manually would be overwritten by auditing on flush. Hmm.

Options: add a `@Version`? No. Simplest: since the spec's If-Match behavior test is: save, then save with stale etag → 412. If identical payload yields same etag, the "stale" etag equals current → my check would accept it (no mismatch). That's fine for the stale test if I use a *different* payload. But to be faithful ("increment updated_on"), I want a genuine change.

I can force a version bump by including timeSpentSeconds in the comparison... still may be identical.

Alternative: set `updatedOn` explicitly to now and annotate with @LastModifiedDate — auditing will set it anyway to now on flush. Since auditing sets it to Instant.now() at flush, the millis will differ from the previous save (unless within same millisecond). Tests run fast, so two saves could land in the same millisecond → identical etag possible but the crc differs if payload differs.

For the stale test I'll use different payloads so etags definitely differ. Good enough and faithful.

Actually, I realize a cleaner approach: track the precondition against `updatedOn` + `crc` (the etag) as done. Fine.

Also: "If-Match matches current ETag → Accept, return new ETag". New etag naturally has a new updatedOn (auditing). Acceptable.

Note: to make the update actually fire, the entity must be dirty. If nothing changed, no UPDATE, updatedOn unchanged. That's acceptable behavior (idempotent save returns the same etag) — arguably correct for idempotency.

- timeSpentSeconds != null && < 0 → ValidationException

EvaluationHandoffService:
```java
@Service
public class EvaluationHandoffService {
  public static final String JOB_TYPE = "EVALUATION";
  public static final String REFERENCE_TYPE = "STUDENT_ATTEMPT";
  private final BackgroundJobRepository jobs;
  private final ObjectMapper objectMapper;

  @Transactional
  public BackgroundJob enqueue(UUID attemptId) {
    var existing = jobs.findByJobTypeAndReferenceTypeAndReferenceId(JOB_TYPE, REFERENCE_TYPE, attemptId);
    if (!existing.isEmpty()) return existing.get(0);
    BackgroundJob job = new BackgroundJob();
    job.setJobType(JOB_TYPE);
    job.setReferenceType(REFERENCE_TYPE);
    job.setReferenceId(attemptId);
    job.setInitiatedBy(CurrentAuditorProvider.SYSTEM_ACTOR_ID);
    job.setStatus(BackgroundJobStatus.QUEUED);
    job.setParameters("{\"attemptId\":\"" + attemptId + "\"}");
    job.setQueuedOn(Instant.now());
    return jobs.save(job);
  }
}
```
Idempotency: existsBy... check. But concurrent submits could both see none → two jobs. Use the DB? No unique constraint on background_job. Guard with the submission conditional transition: only the winner calls enqueue. That's sufficient idempotency (§66 "idempotent trigger strategy").

SubmissionService:
```java
@Service
public class SubmissionService {
  attempts, sessions, handoff, eligibility

  @Transactional
  public SubmitResponse submit(UUID attemptId, UUID userId) {
    StudentAttempt attempt = eligibility.requireOwned(attemptId, userId);
    // idempotent
    if (attempt.getAttemptStatus()==SUBMITTED || EVALUATED) {
      return new SubmitResponse(attemptId, attempt.getAttemptStatus().name(), attempt.getSubmittedOn());
    }
    if (attempt.getAttemptStatus()!=IN_PROGRESS) throw new InvalidStateException("Attempt is not in progress");
    Instant now = Instant.now();
    int updated = attempts.markSubmitted(attemptId, IN_PROGRESS, SUBMITTED, userId, now);
    if (updated == 0) {
      StudentAttempt current = attempts.findById(attemptId).orElseThrow();
      if (current.getAttemptStatus()==SUBMITTED || EVALUATED) return new SubmitResponse(...);
      throw new InvalidStateException("Attempt could not be submitted");
    }
    StudentAttempt submitted = attempts.findById(attemptId).orElseThrow();
    // end or transition session as appropriate (does not double-end)
    sessions.endAfterSubmit(attemptId, now);
    handoff.enqueue(attemptId);
    return new SubmitResponse(attemptId, SUBMITTED.name(), submitted.getSubmittedOn());
  }
}
```

I need a session method to end the active session after submit without submitting the attempt (i.e., just transition session). I'll add `endActiveForAttempt(UUID attemptId, Instant now)` to AssessmentSessionService:
```java
@Transactional
public void endActiveForAttempt(UUID attemptId, Instant now) {
  sessionRepository.findFirstByAttemptIdAndSessionStatusOrderByStartedOnDesc(attemptId, ACTIVE).ifPresent(s -> { s.setSessionStatus(LOGGED_OUT); s.setEndedOn(now); sessionRepository.save(s); });
}
```
Add that method to AssessmentSessionService via edit.

Note "Double session end" is an invalid outcome: endActiveForAttempt only transitions if ACTIVE (finds ACTIVE only) → second submit is idempotent (returns early) and won't call it. Good.

```java
@Transactional(readOnly=true)
public List<DeliveryDtos.AttemptResponse> list(UUID userId, UUID assessmentVersionId, String status, Instant from, Instant to) {
  return attemptRepository.findByUserIdOrderByStartedOnDesc(userId).stream()
    .filter(a -> assessmentVersionId==null || assessmentVersionId.equals(a.getAssessmentVersionId()))
    .filter(a -> status==null || status.equals(a.getAttemptStatus()==null?null:a.getAttemptStatus().name()))
    .filter(a -> from==null || (a.getStartedOn()!=null && !a.getStartedOn().isBefore(from)))
    .filter(a -> to==null || (a.getStartedOn()!=null && !a.getStartedOn().isAfter(to)))
    .map(this::toAttemptResponse)
    .toList();
}

Worker:
```java
package com.company.exam.delivery.worker;
@Component
@ConditionalOnProperty(name = "app.delivery.worker.enabled", havingValue = "true")
public class SnapshotPreparationWorker {
  log; SnapshotPreparationService service;
  @Value("${app.delivery.worker.batch-size:50}") int batchSize;
  @Value("${app.delivery.worker.stale-timeout:PT5M}") Duration staleTimeout;
  @Scheduled(fixedDelayString="${app.delivery.worker.interval:PT30S}", initialDelayString="${app.delivery.worker.initial-delay:PT15S}")
  public void preparePendingAttempts() {
    int reclaimed = service.reclaimStale(staleTimeout);
    int prepared = service.preparePendingBatch(batchSize);
    if (prepared>0 || reclaimed>0) log.info(...);
  }
}
```
Duration @Value from PT5M works with Spring conversion.

DeliveryStartController:
```java
@RestController
@RequestMapping(path="/api/v1/assessment-schedules", produces=APPLICATION_JSON_VALUE)
@Tag(...)
public class DeliveryStartController {
  SnapshotPreparationService preparationService; StartAssessmentService startService;
  @PostMapping("/{scheduleId}/prepare-attempts") @ResponseStatus(ACCEPTED) @PreAuthorize("@authorizationService.hasPermission(authentication,'DELIVERY_PREPARE_ATTEMPTS')")
  public ApiResponse<PrepareResponse> prepare(@PathVariable UUID scheduleId, @AuthenticationPrincipal IdentityPrincipal principal) {
     int created = preparationService.createPendingAttemptsForSchedule(scheduleId, principal.userId());
     // also kick off async preparation by returning QUEUED; the worker will prepare them.
     return ApiResponse.ok("Attempt preparation queued", new PrepareResponse(UUID.randomUUID(), "QUEUED", scheduleId));
  }
  @PostMapping("/{scheduleId}/start")
  public ApiResponse<StartResponse> start(@PathVariable UUID scheduleId, @AuthenticationPrincipal IdentityPrincipal principal) {
     return ApiResponse.ok("Assessment started", startService.start(scheduleId, principal.userId()));
  }
}

public List<ResponseView> list(UUID attemptId, UUID userId) {
  eligibilityService.requireOwned(attemptId, userId);
  return responseRepository.findByAttemptId(attemptId).stream().map(r -> toView(r, etag(r))).toList();
}

262:         String reviewer = reviewerToken();
263:         String admin = adminToken();
264: 
265:         String questionBody = "{\"templateId\":\"" + templateId() + "\",\"examId\":\"" + examId
266:                 + "\",\"subjectId\":\"" + subjectId + "\",\"title\":\"Q\",\"content\":\"What is 2+2?\","

269:                         .contentType(JSON).content(questionBody))
270:                 .andExpect(status().isCreated()).andReturn().getResponse().getContentAsString();
271:         String questionId = JsonPath.read(questionCreated, "$.data.id");
272: 
273:         String versions = mockMvc.perform(get("/api/v1/questions/" + questionId + "/versions")
274:                         .header(AUTHORIZATION, bearer(staff)))
275:                 .andReturn().getResponse().getContentAsString();
276:         String versionId = JsonPath.read(versions, "$.data[0].id");
277: 
278:         addOption(staff, questionId, versionId, "A", 1, true);
279:         addOption(staff, questionId, versionId, "B", 2, false);
280: 
281:         mockMvc.perform(post("/api/v1/questions/" + questionId + "/versions/" + versionId + "/submit-review")
282:                 .header(AUTHORIZATION, bearer(staff))).andExpect(status().isOk());
283:         mockMvc.perform(post("/api/v1/questions/" + questionId + "/versions/" + versionId + "/approve")
284:                         .header(AUTHORIZATION, bearer(reviewer)).contentType(JSON).content("{\"remarks\":\"ok\"}"))
285:                 .andExpect(status().isOk());
286:         mockMvc.perform(post("/api/v1/questions/" + questionId + "/versions/" + versionId + "/publish")
287:                 .header(AUTHORIZATION, bearer(admin))).andExpect(status().isOk());
288:         return versionId;
289:     }

```java
@Test void startExamPerformsZeroSnapshotInserts() throws Exception {
  String versionId = publishAssessmentVersion(2);
  String scheduleId = createSchedule(versionId, "EXAM");
  User student = newStudent("p5-start");
  prepareAttempts(scheduleId);
  UUID attemptId = attemptIdFor(UUID.fromString(scheduleId), student.getId());
  long before = countAttemptQuestions(attemptId);
  assertThat(before).isEqualTo(2);
  long optionsBefore = count of attempt_question_option;
  // start
  String token = tokenFor(student);
  String body = mockMvc.perform(post("/api/v1/assessment-schedules/"+scheduleId+"/start").header(AUTHORIZATION, bearer(token)))
     .andExpect(status().isOk())
     .andExpect(jsonPath("$.data.attemptId").value(attemptId.toString()))
     .andExpect(jsonPath("$.data.mode").value("EXAM"))
     .andReturn().getResponse().getContentAsString();
  assertThat(countAttemptQuestions(attemptId)).isEqualTo(2);
  assertThat(countAttemptOptions(attemptId)).isEqualTo(optionsBefore);
  // session created exactly once
  assertThat(countSessions(attemptId)).isEqualTo(1);
  // idempotent repeat
  mockMvc.perform(post(...start...).header(...)).andExpect(status().isOk());
  assertThat(countSessions(attemptId)).isEqualTo(1);
  assertThat(countAttemptQuestions(attemptId)).isEqualTo(2);
}
```

```java
@Test void staleWriteIsRejectedWith412() throws Exception {
  setup started attempt + questions
  // GET a questionVersionId from GET /attempts/{id}/questions
  // first save (no If-Match) → 200 + ETag header
  // second save with that ETag and different payload → 200 + new ETag
  // third save with the OLD etag → 412 STALE_RESPONSE
}
```
Need questionVersionId: from start response? Start response has no questionVersionId. Use GET /attempts/{id}/questions → data[0].questionVersionId.

First save: PUT with no If-Match → 200. Get ETag header from response.
Second save with If-Match=<etag1>, payload response "B" → 200, etag2 (from header).
Third save with If-Match=<etag1> (stale) → 412 with errors[0].code STALE_RESPONSE.

@Test void concurrentStartBurstAcrossManyUsersIsRaceSafe() throws Exception {
  version, schedule, 10 students created, prepareAttempts, 10 threads each start distinct student
  assert all succeeded (200 / no exception), each attempt has 1 session, snapshot rows unchanged (2 per attempt)
}
```

For concurrency I'll use ExecutorService + CountDownLatch.

Note: startAssessmentService.start is @Transactional; calling from many threads creates many txs. The embedded Postgres is a single instance; fine.

Potential issue: with `markStarted` conditional update, concurrent starts on the same attempt: each thread does a SELECT (candidates) then UPDATE ... WHERE attempt_status=NOT_STARTED. In READ COMMITTED, thread A updates+commits; thread B's UPDATE blocks until A commits, then re-evaluates the WHERE on the updated row → 0 rows; then B re-reads → IN_PROGRESS → returns established. Good, race-safe. But B's earlier read may have seen NOT_STARTED; after update 0 rows, we re-read (findById) → but the tx snapshot in READ COMMITTED sees A's committed change. Good.

However, activateOrCreate: thread A creates session; thread B may also create if it read before A committed. In the same-attempt case, B goes through the `alreadyStarted` path (found IN_PROGRESS from candidates? candidates were read at the start — B read candidates possibly before A started → sees NOT_STARTED, not IN_PROGRESS). Then B proceeds to ready (READY+NOT_STARTED), markStarted → blocks, 0 rows, then re-reads attempt (IN_PROGRESS) and calls established() which calls activateOrCreate → finds A's ACTIVE session (committed) → reuses. Good, so 1 session.

But if B's read of candidates happened after A committed, B sees IN_PROGRESS → established() → reuses session. Good.

Edge: B could call activateOrCreate before A commits its session insert → both insert sessions → 2 sessions. Since A's markStarted commits before A creates the session? No—all in ONE transaction: A's tx includes markStarted + session insert + commit. So B's markStarted blocks on A's row lock until A commits (which includes the session insert). Then B re-reads and finds the session. So 1 session. 

But wait: within A's tx, markStarted is a bulk update on student_attempt; the row lock is held until commit. B's UPDATE on the same row blocks. Correct.

However B's `candidates` read (findByAssessmentScheduleIdAndUserId) happens BEFORE its update and does NOT block. Fine.

Now the multi-user burst: distinct attempts, no contention. All succeed.

Also: `eligibility.requireOpenSchedule` calls assessment.requireSchedule (read). Fine.

collect results as List<DeliveryDtos.StartResponse> and exceptions list.

Implementation:
```java
private List<DeliveryDtos.StartResponse> runConcurrently(int n, IntFunction<DeliveryDtos.StartResponse> task) throws Exception {
  ExecutorService pool = Executors.newFixedThreadPool(n);
  CountDownLatch ready = new CountDownLatch(n), go = new CountDownLatch(1);
  List<Future<DeliveryDtos.StartResponse>> futures = new ArrayList<>();
  for (int i=0;i<n;i++){ final int idx=i; futures.add(pool.submit(() -> { ready.countDown(); go.await(); return task.apply(idx); })); }
  ready.await(); go.countDown();
  List<DeliveryDtos.StartResponse> out = new ArrayList<>();
  for (Future<...> f : futures) out.add(f.get(30, SECONDS));
  pool.shutdown();
  return out;
}
```
f.get throws ExecutionException if the task threw → test fails with the cause. For the same-attempt test I want all to succeed; my service returns established context for losers, so none throw. Good.
